# The Role of Islam in the End Times

**[00:00:00]** Islam is a massive religion with over a billion members today.

**[00:00:03]** It has a very interesting history, and it's also dominant in the Middle East,

**[00:00:07]** where many falsely believe it is the center of Bible prophecy.

**[00:00:11]** But what exactly will the role of Islam be in the end times, according to the Bible?

**[00:00:16]** That's our topic on today's episode of The Dance of Life.
